# Additive tuning of 2026-11-07, mcp round 2 variant 4, scenario 5

## Run identity

```yaml
model: claude-sonnet-5
served_version: claude-sonnet-5
tree: sha256:a7bde7d037f359894dc19c4a6c209888291ec5716ca604a048b513eb82768c69
lock: sha256:826dcf9f83c5d9e7fb557d7a1df9753a09a5c70c645d6c9faf7a575256119178
selection: sha256:d99530b2b639bb6bdd279be2a9356a64ce2a0c8cceca354d56957f198a76a20a
read_set: sha256:2a47d5b0401ca6d3239d352781fe101f4d7ba74c60c6a0dbe460e46ae9703c4d
seed: 0
harness: 0.6.1
tier: additive
arm: scenario-5
at: 2026-11-07
cost_cents: 1753
```

**This line is the `screen 4` stage of round 2 of the MCP layer, in the tuning round of the additive campaign that [#1880](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880) asked for.** A screen measures one variant on a small batch, so that the round can rank the variants before it pays for a confirmation. Variant 4 is screened from the pushed branch `tune/mcp-r2-v4`, on `644475c9`, the commit that opens round 2 under the owner's ruling ([comment 6701580649](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880#issuecomment-6701580649)). `tools/probe/try.sh launch` ran it from the one-line spec `additive scenario-5 tuning`. It is the `scenario-5` arm of the additive tier over the 22 probes of the tuning set, at 1 repetition per probe, on `claude-sonnet-5` under Claude Code 2.1.288.

**What the variant changes.** Variant 4 combines variant 1, variant 2 and variant 3, with the `check` of variant 2. Variant 3 names where an identifier is claimed, and how a new document gets a claim. The change is only in `engine/crates/query/src/mcp.rs`, as the [candidates of the round](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880#issuecomment-6702132636) require.

**One tree, one pin, one launch.** Every session ran in a fresh copy of a `git archive` of the pinned tree, and the engine of that tree. The pin was `6dfda377` for the whole batch. The canary of 8 sessions cost 827 cents, and the owner read its records and [passed it](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880#issuecomment-6712870651). The `tree` digest in the identity above is the digest the assembly recorded.

**What the recorder counted.** The line holds 22 sessions and cost 1753 cents: the intent hook live in 0, 1 stopped at the turn cap, 0 stopped at the session budget, 16 paths outside the workspace named, 0 sessions uncounted, 0 web-tool calls. 15 of the 22 sessions used the MCP layer. A session the recorder could not count adds nothing to either count, so each count is a floor.

**The result.** Variant 4 passed 18 of 22 graded sessions. The base row compares this measurement with the base, scenario 2, which passed 112 of 154 in [the base](additive-tuning-of-2026-11-02-engine-round-1-best-so-far-scenario-2.md). The difference is +9.1 points, with the interval [-12.2, +22.3]. The interval includes zero, and 22 sessions are enough to rank the variants and not enough to keep one. A screen is never kept, and only a confirmation is (owner, 2026-10-22, #1777).

**Where the variant ranks.** The owner ruled that round 2 ranks on passes, because the round aims at passes and not at use ([comment 6712870651](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880#issuecomment-6712870651)). A tie goes to the lower cost for each session, and the gate on the use of the layer does not apply to this round ([comment 6713732364](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880#issuecomment-6713732364)). The screens rank in this order: variant 2 (18 passed, 12 used, 1727 cents), variant 4 (18 passed, 15 used, 1753 cents) and variant 1 (17 passed, 14 used, 2061 cents). The owner voided variant 3 after its canary ([comment 6705973633](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1880#issuecomment-6705973633)). Variant 2 ranks first, and it goes on to the confirmation of round 2, at 154 sessions.

**The contract of this batch.** The sessions ran under the recorder contract of spec 15: a `bwrap` sandbox with no network of its own, a configuration directory that holds a copy of the credentials and nothing else, the permission mode `dontAsk`, and `WebSearch` and `WebFetch` denied. The owner approved $1,300.00 for this layer. The driver ran this try with a cap of 20000 cents and a session budget of 300 cents.

**What the batch cost.** The driver charged 1753 cents against its cap. A screen is charged to the tuning ceiling of 100000 cents.

**Where the raw recordings are.** The raw session logs are outside git, under `/mnt/build/hw-1880/mcp-r2-v4/a/sessions/L1-*/raw.jsonl` on the host that ran the batch. Nobody deletes them until the report of [#1779](https://github.com/headwater-ai/headwater/issues/1779) is published.

**Where the other figures are.** The rate and the interval of each probe are in the generated result that reads this transcript.
